# The game's multiplayer announcer (tools/engine_announcer_reference.py).
# game <game type> <teams> <whether the globals have their multiplayer information>
# sound <index, or -1 for a missing sound> <its longest permutation, milliseconds>
# call <game tick> <sound>: the game asks for a sound
# play <game tick> <sound>: a sound plays
# end <game ticks>
